I've made extensive fixes to the mcJava project. Here's a summary of what was accomplished:

## Fixes Applied

### 1. **Invalid `instanceof` Patterns Fixed**
- Fixed incorrectly converted switch expressions that produced invalid syntax like `if (var instanceof A, B)`

### 2. **Pattern Matching Switches on Sealed Types Converted**
- Converted to if-else chains in multiple files to avoid MatchException requirement
- Fixed in: `ServerLevel.java`, `Entity.java`, `CollisionContext.java`, `DamageSource.java`, `LivingEntity.java`

### 3. **Missing Variable Bindings Restored**
- Fixed pattern variable bindings in `Entity.java` (5 locations), `EntityType.java`, `Block.java` (3 locations), `ServerLevel.java` (3 locations)

### 4. **Syntax Errors Fixed**
- Missing semicolons after throw statements
- Corrupted lambda expressions
- Incomplete if-else branches
- Fixed `keymap.java` (KEYCODE_END → KEYCODE_MOVE_END)
- Fixed `SectionCategory.java` and `MemoryPool.java` stub files

### 5. **Created jtracy Stubs**
- Added no-op implementations for `TracyClient`, `Zone`, `DiscontinuousFrame`, `Plot` in `com/mojang/jtracy/`

### 6. **Updated Build Configuration**
- Added exclusions for: `net/minecraft/client/**`, `com/mojang/blaze3d/**`, `com/mojang/renderpearl/**`, `com/mojang/realmsclient/**`, `com/mojang/text2speech/**`, `org/lwjgl/**`, `net/minecraft/realms/**`, `net/fabricmc/**`, `stubs/**`, `pro/minecraft/**`

## Remaining Blocker: MatchException

The build fails with `com.sun.tools.javac.code.Symbol$CompletionFailure: class file for java.lang.MatchException not found`. This is a fundamental limitation:

- **Cause**: JDK 21 compiler requires `java.lang.MatchException` for pattern matching switches on sealed types
- **Problem**: This class doesn't exist in Android's java.base module
- **Constraint**: java.lang is a sealed module - cannot add classes via boot classpath, patch-module, or custom JAR

## Recommended Next Steps

As noted in the GitHub issue, the only viable solutions are:
1. **Custom JDK**: Build a custom JDK with MatchException added to java.base
2. **Complete Conversion**: Use automated tools (OpenRewrite/Error Prone) to convert ALL pattern matching switches on sealed types to if-else chains in both source code AND dependencies
3. **Target Desktop**: Build for desktop JDK 21+ instead of Android
4. **Android 14+**: Use Android 14 (UpsideDownCake) which has better Java 21 support

The project now has significantly fewer compilation errors, but the MatchException issue requires either a custom runtime environment or complete elimination of pattern matching on sealed types from the entire dependency chain.
